# OSDS — Scientific Reference Register

**Observational Systems and Data Sources** · Work in progress

A scientific discovery register of observation networks, monitoring infrastructure, archives and data platforms. All **62 entries are candidates**: coverage and observation-category assignments remain unverified, and no complete source record or dataset has been independently verified. The authoritative [machine reference](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md) provides detailed classifications, evidence and unresolved research questions.

## Reading key

The authoritative inventory is the assessment baseline. Incomplete historical
research context does not establish complete source coverage or new adopted
classifications. Record-level candidate status, inherited domain/category
assertions and dated discovery-URL identity evidence are separate: none verifies
measurement quality, product fitness, rights or every dataset from a provider.
Nulls and empty arrays record unknown/unassigned information, not confirmed absence.
The registers now use document version 0.2.0. The preceding documentary
maintenance revision `2026-11-02-documentary` is preserved as history; new research
is a separately typed candidate extension, not a change to the source tables.

Source identifiers are SRC-001–SRC-062, qualified as `OSDS:SRC-001` across projects. D01–D08 and O01–O08 are OSDS-specific classifications, not approved DRM-wide codes or equivalents of EII's domain codes. Sources may belong to several domains and categories; the tables group them by their primary presentation domain. Observation descriptions express the recorded category assignments, not independently verified measurement capabilities. Each OSDS ID links to its machine record, and each source name links to its official discovery page.

| Code | Observation category |
|---|---|
| O01 | IoT telemetry |
| O02 | Scientific monitoring |
| O03 | Ground-truth observations |
| O04 | Autonomous observations |
| O05 | Campaign datasets |
| O06 | Citizen sensing |
| O07 | Physical computing |
| O08 | Historical instrumental records |

[Candidate research expansion](#candidate-research-expansion--document-020): instrumentation, methods, computing, themes, assertions and qualified evidence.

## Discovery links and access limitations — 22 October 2026

Links identify official sources or discovery pages; they do not establish dataset availability or quality. Some checks used indexed official pages where direct access was blocked. OSCAR/Surface displayed a test-environment notice and its operational link redirected to the same page; ISC displayed a temporary outage notice. Lincoln is a collection of agricultural datasets, AG-LOAM resolves to its maintainer's dataset documentation, and the historical NREL label now links to a current NLR grid data/tools page. Details and evidence are retained per record in the machine register.

## Scientific interpretation

These are discovery routes, not interchangeable measurement datasets. Station and site registries may expose metadata; repositories may hold heterogeneous research outputs; coordinated services may combine observations, estimates and models. Category membership does not establish that a source supplies calibrated measurements or open data. Select a specific product and inspect its method, units, quality, time span and rights before analytical use.

Preserved entries with possible overlap include ICOS / ICOS Ecosystem Stations (005/040), EarthScope / EarthScope Research Infrastructure (022/062), Zenodo / Zenodo Research Datasets (055/058), and NASA Open Data / NASA Earthdata (053/057). These remain separate pending identity and scope review. Agricultural placement of EFAS and the scope of broad repository entries also require review.

## Foundational standards and interoperability

[SOSA / SSN — Semantic Sensor Network Ontology](https://www.w3.org/TR/vocab-ssn-2017/), [OGC SensorThings API](https://www.ogc.org/standards/sensorthings/), and [OGC Observations, Measurements and Samples (OMS)](https://www.ogc.org/standards/om/) are reference foundations for later entity and access modelling. Editions, formal mappings and implementation claims remain unverified.

## Candidate research expansion — document 0.2.0

The original 62-source inventory and its 220 memberships are unchanged. The
following are separately typed **documentary candidates**, not new providers,
verified instruments, adopted families, approved scope exclusions or implemented
services. A01 is a retrospective reconstruction with bounded access; A02 supplies
richer audit-reported details and user-decision claims without an authenticated
original transcript. They are complementary, not independent confirmation.
Original scientific turns remain unavailable through the bounded later excerpt.

Documentary inclusion, canonical candidate state, identity, scientific review,
reported authority, relationship review and rights/access are independent.
Every new item remains unverified; a reported “confirmed” decision is not an
authenticated approval. This is author-performed documentary semantic review,
not independent expert review or scientific verification. Source-specific units,
calibration, model performance, standard editions, rights and scope need review.

### Campaign datasets

[OSDS:RC-0021](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#rc-0021) · observation method context · candidate; identity/science unverified.

**R§7; N§5.5.** Time-bounded scientific campaigns, field surveys, sampling events and systematic observation logging are explicit research subjects. Ecological surveys and species-sighting records are included. The conceptual sequence is preserved exactly:

### Raspberry Pi 5

[OSDS:RC-0040](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#rc-0040) · computing platform candidate · candidate; identity/science unverified.

Embedded controllers, microcontrollers, single-board computers, DAQ systems, sensor interfaces, communications, edge processing, field fabrication and open hardware. IoT asks how data are communicated; physical computing asks how signals are acquired, interpreted and acted upon. An offline logger is physical computing without IoT; a connected commercial meter may be both. Named hardware: NVIDIA Jetson Nano and Orin Nano, LicheeRV Nano (RISC-V), ESP32-S3, Raspberry Pi 5; capabilities include edge inference, computer vision, segmentation, object detection, acoustic classification, sensor fusion, adaptive sampling and autonomy. Actual board specs and model performance were not verified. Digital Naturalism Laboratories inspired field-adaptive/open scientific instrumentation, wearable and interactive tools, in-situ fabrication, field modification logs, context preservation and ecological validity. OS Beehives/OSBH introduced bioacoustic recording, feature extraction, feedback and biological inference boundary. Technical leads: Arduino, PlatformIO, GitHub, Hackaday, Public Lab, WILDLABS, MQTT, LoRaWAN, SDI-12, OSHWA and SensorThings.

Complete source fields and additional qualified accounts: [OSDS:OR-0073](#evidence-or-0073).

### Bioacoustics

[OSDS:RC-0081](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#rc-0081) · method capability candidate · candidate; identity/science unverified.

**A — scope tensions requiring review:** Does “aerial” exclude aircraft atmospheric observations, or aerial mapping specifically? How are autonomous ocean floats, underwater/subsurface systems, laboratory and industrial measurements treated? Which biological instrumentation is specialised while camera trapping, bioacoustics and beehive sensing remain relevant? How are mixed providers handled at product level? Existing IAGOS, Argo, NASA, Copernicus and GBIF records must retain their identities and history while those questions are assessed. Location metadata and use of GIS in analysis do not by themselves settle catalogue ownership.

Complete source fields and additional qualified accounts: [OSDS:OR-0143](#evidence-or-0143), [OSDS:OR-0060](#evidence-or-0060), [OSDS:OR-0142](#evidence-or-0142), [OSDS:OR-0079](#evidence-or-0079).

N additionally proposes raw-input, model/version, annotation, threshold, uncertainty and human-review evidence. These are candidate pipeline descriptions. A recording, extracted feature, model output and ecological interpretation must remain distinguishable; no bee-colony condition, species inference or indicator is validated by this audit.

Complete source fields and additional qualified accounts: [OSDS:OR-0060](#evidence-or-0060), [OSDS:OR-0142](#evidence-or-0142).

### Camera trapping

[OSDS:RC-0083](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#rc-0083) · method capability candidate · candidate; identity/science unverified.

**A — scope tensions requiring review:** Does “aerial” exclude aircraft atmospheric observations, or aerial mapping specifically? How are autonomous ocean floats, underwater/subsurface systems, laboratory and industrial measurements treated? Which biological instrumentation is specialised while camera trapping, bioacoustics and beehive sensing remain relevant? How are mixed providers handled at product level? Existing IAGOS, Argo, NASA, Copernicus and GBIF records must retain their identities and history while those questions are assessed. Location metadata and use of GIS in analysis do not by themselves settle catalogue ownership.

Complete source fields and additional qualified accounts: [OSDS:OR-0143](#evidence-or-0143), [OSDS:OR-0142](#evidence-or-0142), [OSDS:OR-0079](#evidence-or-0079).

### Adaptive sampling

[OSDS:RC-0088](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#rc-0088) · method capability candidate · candidate; identity/science unverified.

Embedded controllers, microcontrollers, single-board computers, DAQ systems, sensor interfaces, communications, edge processing, field fabrication and open hardware. IoT asks how data are communicated; physical computing asks how signals are acquired, interpreted and acted upon. An offline logger is physical computing without IoT; a connected commercial meter may be both. Named hardware: NVIDIA Jetson Nano and Orin Nano, LicheeRV Nano (RISC-V), ESP32-S3, Raspberry Pi 5; capabilities include edge inference, computer vision, segmentation, object detection, acoustic classification, sensor fusion, adaptive sampling and autonomy. Actual board specs and model performance were not verified. Digital Naturalism Laboratories inspired field-adaptive/open scientific instrumentation, wearable and interactive tools, in-situ fabrication, field modification logs, context preservation and ecological validity. OS Beehives/OSBH introduced bioacoustic recording, feature extraction, feedback and biological inference boundary. Technical leads: Arduino, PlatformIO, GitHub, Hackaday, Public Lab, WILDLABS, MQTT, LoRaWAN, SDI-12, OSHWA and SensorThings.

Complete source fields and additional qualified accounts: [OSDS:OR-0073](#evidence-or-0073), [OSDS:OR-0079](#evidence-or-0079).

### Protocols

[OSDS:RC-0089](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#rc-0089) · method capability candidate · candidate; identity/science unverified.

Community/school/volunteer measurement with low-cost air sensors, DIY weather stations, soil and water meters and noise monitors. Examples: Sensor.Community, openSenseMap, Smart Citizen, PurpleAir, OpenAQ, GLOBE and FreshWater Watch. Framework leads: ECSA principles, GLOBE protocols, EPA Air Sensor Toolbox and Darwin Core for biological observations. Benefits: hyper-local coverage and participation; challenges: calibration, placement, training, quality assurance and comparability. WILDLABS is a discovery/community gateway for conservation technologies, not assumed a direct observation provider.

Complete source fields and additional qualified accounts: [OSDS:OR-0072](#evidence-or-0072), [OSDS:OR-0079](#evidence-or-0079).

## Qualified assertions and research questions

Original subject/predicate/object and authority wording are retained as reported
assertions, with separate current qualifications. “OSDS measures” refers to the
physical systems described, not to measurements performed by this catalogue.
A02’s provisional theme assignments do not overwrite A01’s lack of assignments.
Aircraft/marine/subsurface/biological boundaries and proposed BIPKB naming are
unresolved; no provider is removed or external namespace adopted. The original
three foundational references remain separate from later PROV-O prompt-origin
material. Conceptual arrows do not establish causation or operational bindings.

### 1. OSDS purpose, scope and boundaries

[OSDS:RA-0001](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#ra-0001) · scope method or evidence qualification · proposed; science unverified; authority audit-reported.

The reported division places satellite, aerial and Earth-observation mapping primarily with GISDS; specialised biological instrumentation and biological evidence primarily with a future biological database. Interdisciplinary links should preserve relationships without duplicating another database's authority. These are proposed scientific responsibility boundaries to reconcile, not automatic deletion rules.

**Qualification:** Retained as source-reported proposal/analysis, not authenticated original dialogue or adopted scope exclusion.

Full supporting context: [OSDS:OR-0152](#evidence-or-0152).

### 11. Instrumentation and technology families across categories

[OSDS:RA-0002](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#ra-0002) · scope method or evidence qualification · proposed; science unverified; authority audit-reported.

**R§11; A design analysis.** Consolidation should avoid duplicating one instrument merely because it participates in multiple observation categories. Distinguish instrument identity, computing platform, deployment platform, measurement principle, property, processing capability, source organisation and observation product.

**Qualification:** Retained as source-reported proposal/analysis, not authenticated original dialogue or adopted scope exclusion.

Full supporting context: [OSDS:OR-0153](#evidence-or-0153).

### 11. Instrumentation and technology families across categories

[OSDS:RA-0003](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#ra-0003) · scope method or evidence qualification · proposed; science unverified; authority audit-reported.

The sixteen labels in §3 are family candidates. The five platform names in §9 are identity leads. Embedded AI/edge inference is a capability-family proposal. Acoustic/optical recording, model processing and ecological interpretation are separate levels. No fully specified instrument instance is recovered from R.

**Qualification:** Retained as source-reported proposal/analysis, not authenticated original dialogue or adopted scope exclusion.

Full supporting context: [OSDS:OR-0154](#evidence-or-0154).

### 12. Methodologies, frameworks and standards

[OSDS:RA-0004](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#ra-0004) · scope method or evidence qualification · proposed; science unverified; authority audit-reported.

No new authors, titles, DOIs, URLs, standard editions, licences or compliance assertions are fabricated. Existing foundation URLs remain in the machine register with their dated evidence. R/N supply names, not a complete bibliography. Reference identity, relevance, implementation and claim support require distinct review.

**Qualification:** Retained as source-reported proposal/analysis, not authenticated original dialogue or adopted scope exclusion.

Full supporting context: [OSDS:OR-0155](#evidence-or-0155).

### 13. Cross-cutting research themes

[OSDS:RA-0006](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#ra-0006) · scope method or evidence qualification · proposed; science unverified; authority audit-reported.

N's four different lenses—measurement trust; embedded computation; participation/situated practice; temporal continuity—were explicitly its author's editorial prompts, not recovered original umbrellas. They must not be merged with, or silently substituted for, R's four labels.

### Observational technologies / Camera trapping

[OSDS:RA-0135](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#ra-0135) · theme membership proposal · proposed; science unverified; authority audit-reported.

**Subject:** OSDS:RC-0083. **Predicate:** proposed_theme_association. **Object:** OSDS:RC-0025.

Four **proposed theme umbrellas**, explicitly accepted only as an approach to record, not as adopted taxonomy: **observational technologies** (camera trapping, acoustic monitoring); **computational interpretation** (computer vision, AI for conservation, audio analysis); **embedded intelligence** (edge AI, local processing, adaptive sampling); **scientific methods and interoperability** (field laboratories, calibration, protocols, historical reconstruction). Bioacoustics and camera traps bridge OSDS capture systems with the biological database; biological inference is not automatically OSDS-owned. WILDLABS categorises around technologies, conservation challenges, ecosystems and organisations; OSDS categorises around observation modes, domains, instruments, methods and indicators. This comparison was conversational, not independently verified against a current WILDLABS taxonomy.

**Qualification:** A02 proposes this association; A01 supplies no assignment. It is not an adopted taxonomy or original source-category membership.

Full supporting context: [OSDS:OR-0079](#evidence-or-0079).

**Subject:** OSDS:RC-0068. **Predicate:** proposed_theme_association. **Object:** OSDS:RC-0028.

Four **proposed theme umbrellas**, explicitly accepted only as an approach to record, not as adopted taxonomy: **observational technologies** (camera trapping, acoustic monitoring); **computational interpretation** (computer vision, AI for conservation, audio analysis); **embedded intelligence** (edge AI, local processing, adaptive sampling); **scientific methods and interoperability** (field laboratories, calibration, protocols, historical reconstruction). Bioacoustics and camera traps bridge OSDS capture systems with the biological database; biological inference is not automatically OSDS-owned. WILDLABS categorises around technologies, conservation challenges, ecosystems and organisations; OSDS categorises around observation modes, domains, instruments, methods and indicators. This comparison was conversational, not independently verified against a current WILDLABS taxonomy.

**Qualification:** A02 proposes this association; A01 supplies no assignment. It is not an adopted taxonomy or original source-category membership.

Full supporting context: [OSDS:OR-0079](#evidence-or-0079).

### Biological namespace

[OSDS:RA-0151](OSDS_Machine_Reference.md#ra-0151) · correction or conflict · proposed; science unverified; authority audit-reported.

The reported division places satellite, aerial and Earth-observation mapping primarily with GISDS; specialised biological instrumentation and biological evidence primarily with a future biological database. Interdisciplinary links should preserve relationships without duplicating another database's authority. These are proposed scientific responsibility boundaries to reconcile, not automatic deletion rules.

**Qualification:** BIPKB occurs as an audit-reported future biological database suggestion. No namespace adoption, source deletion or cross-repository mapping is authorised.

Full supporting context: [OSDS:OR-0152](#evidence-or-0152), [OSDS:OR-0166](#evidence-or-0166), [OSDS:OR-0196](#evidence-or-0196).

A **validation role**, not a distinct instrument universe. Soil gravimetric sampling (wet/dry mass), pH/nutrient laboratory assays, manual water-quality tests, stream gauging, portable reference meteorology, field spectroradiometry and geotechnical penetrometers/shear tests. Time-bounded surveys, expeditions, integrated field campaigns, soil/irrigation surveys, hydrological events, atmospheric, geophysical and urban surveys. Methods include transects, timed counts, repeated plots, sample events and manual observations. Instruments include corers, portable sondes, resistivity meters, thermal cameras and flow meters. Sources: NEON, USDA ARS, USGS, PANGAEA, Zenodo, Environmental Data Initiative, DataONE. For moth sighting, distinguish sampling protocol/effort, event metadata, occurrence, identification evidence and verification. Darwin Core, Humboldt Extension and GBIF event-oriented publication were named; none makes an observation automatically scientifically valid. Campaign→event→observation→verification is a useful working abstraction, not a universal normative workflow.

Community/school/volunteer measurement with low-cost air sensors, DIY weather stations, soil and water meters and noise monitors. Examples: Sensor.Community, openSenseMap, Smart Citizen, PurpleAir, OpenAQ, GLOBE and FreshWater Watch. Framework leads: ECSA principles, GLOBE protocols, EPA Air Sensor Toolbox and Darwin Core for biological observations. Benefits: hyper-local coverage and participation; challenges: calibration, placement, training, quality assurance and comparability. WILDLABS is a discovery/community gateway for conservation technologies, not assumed a direct observation provider.

**Reported authority context:** audit author account; original scientific message locator unavailable; not authenticated. Source authority tokens remain reports, not current acceptance.

Embedded controllers, microcontrollers, single-board computers, DAQ systems, sensor interfaces, communications, edge processing, field fabrication and open hardware. IoT asks how data are communicated; physical computing asks how signals are acquired, interpreted and acted upon. An offline logger is physical computing without IoT; a connected commercial meter may be both. Named hardware: NVIDIA Jetson Nano and Orin Nano, LicheeRV Nano (RISC-V), ESP32-S3, Raspberry Pi 5; capabilities include edge inference, computer vision, segmentation, object detection, acoustic classification, sensor fusion, adaptive sampling and autonomy. Actual board specs and model performance were not verified. Digital Naturalism Laboratories inspired field-adaptive/open scientific instrumentation, wearable and interactive tools, in-situ fabrication, field modification logs, context preservation and ecological validity. OS Beehives/OSBH introduced bioacoustic recording, feature extraction, feedback and biological inference boundary. Technical leads: Arduino, PlatformIO, GitHub, Hackaday, Public Lab, WILDLABS, MQTT, LoRaWAN, SDI-12, OSHWA and SensorThings.

Four **proposed theme umbrellas**, explicitly accepted only as an approach to record, not as adopted taxonomy: **observational technologies** (camera trapping, acoustic monitoring); **computational interpretation** (computer vision, AI for conservation, audio analysis); **embedded intelligence** (edge AI, local processing, adaptive sampling); **scientific methods and interoperability** (field laboratories, calibration, protocols, historical reconstruction). Bioacoustics and camera traps bridge OSDS capture systems with the biological database; biological inference is not automatically OSDS-owned. WILDLABS categorises around technologies, conservation challenges, ecosystems and organisations; OSDS categorises around observation modes, domains, instruments, methods and indicators. This comparison was conversational, not independently verified against a current WILDLABS taxonomy.
